## GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem · 匯總

**id**　972f6492ae0d13fded-1

**english**　GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 空間參考和信息

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html

**text**　GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem 對象可生成一個具有自定義投影座標系統的 SpatialReference 對象，該座標系最適用于地圖或分析的指定范圍和預期目的。
由于無法將曲面投影到沒有變形的平面上，因此存在各種在保留角度、面積、距離或方向之間折衷的地圖投影。 地圖的預期目的和范圍可以幫助確定要保留這些屬性中的哪些屬性。 例如，最好為導航地圖選擇等角投影以保持方位角，或者可能需要投影保持面積以可視化或測量圖徵的相對大小。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html#summary

## GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem · 語法

**id**　972f6492ae0d13fded-4

**english**　GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 空間參考和信息

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- property
- Specifies the property that will be used to represent the purpose of the projection. EQUAL_AREA—The relative area of regions everywhere on earth will be preserved. Shape and distances will be distorted. CONFORMAL—Angles in small areas will be preserved, so shapes, for the most part, will be preserved. Size and distances will be distorted. EQUIDISTANT_ONE_POINT—Distance will be preserved when measured through the center of the projection. Area, shape, and other distances will be distorted. EQUIDISTANT_MERIDIANS—Distance will be preserved when measured along meridians. Area, shape, and other distances will be distorted. COMPROMISE_WORLD—Area, shape, and distance will not be specifically preserved, but a balance between these geometric properties will be created. Compromise projections are only suggested for very large areas. Not all properties are supported for all extents of interest. The CONFORMAL and EQUIDISTANT_MERIDIANS properties are only supported for large-scale extents, and the COMPROMISE_WORLD property is only supported for full-world extents. The EQUAL_AREA and EQUIDISTANT_ONE_POINT properties are supported for all extents
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html#syntax

**id**　972f6492ae0d13fded-7

**text**　創建自定義投影座標系統，以在圖徵類的范圍內優化保留面積，然后將圖徵類投影到自定義投影座標系統。

**content_version**　3.7

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html#code-sample

## GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem · 代碼示例

**id**　972f6492ae0d13fded-8

**english**　GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 空間參考和信息

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html

**text**　import arcpy

# Input features in Projected Coordinate System
infc = r"C:\Projected.gdb\Colorado_Counties"

# Get extent of features
desc = arcpy.Describe(infc)
extent = desc.extent

# Project the extent to Geographic Coordinate System WGS84
gcs_sr = arcpy.SpatialReference(4326)
prj_extent = extent.projectAs(gcs_sr)

# Create custom projected coordinate system
custom_sr = arcpy.GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem(prj_extent, "EQUAL_AREA",
                                                  "EQUAL_AREA_COLORADO")

# Project the input features to the custom projection
fc_prj = arcpy.management.Project(infc, "TestPoly_Projected", custom_sr)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html#code-sample

## GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem · 代碼示例

**id**　972f6492ae0d13fded-10

**english**　GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 空間參考和信息

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html

**text**　import arcpy

gcs_sr = arcpy.SpatialReference(4326) # Create WGS84 spatial reference

# Create polygon
poly_info = [[20, 20], [20, 60], [60, 60], [60, 20], [20, 20]]
poly_pts = arcpy.Array([arcpy.Point(*coords) for coords in poly_info])
poly = arcpy.Polygon(poly_pts, gcs_sr)

# Create extent object from polygon
extent = poly.extent

# Create custom projected coordinate system
custom_sr = arcpy.GenerateOptimalCoordinateSystem(extent, "CONFORMAL")

# Project the polygon to the custom projection
poly_prj = poly.projectAs(custom_sr)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/generateoptimalcoordinatesystem.html#code-sample

**english**　Control license behavior

**chapter**　
- Python
- 地理處理和 Python
- 創建地理處理工具
- 在自定義工具箱中創建腳本工具
- 自定義帶有驗證功能的腳本工具行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geoprocessing_and_python/controlling-license-behavior.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geoprocessing_and_python/controlling-license-behavior.html

**text**　isLicensed 方法是一種可選驗證方法，用于檢查腳本工具是否具有運行許可。 如果運行其他地理處理工具（由腳本工具使用）所需的相應許可和擴展模塊不可用，那么可使用該方法限制工具的運行。
如果 isLicensed 方法返回 False，則工具無法運行。 如果該方法返回 True 或者未使用該方法，則工具可以運行。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/geoprocessing_and_python/controlling-license-behavior.html

**english**　Control license behavior

- 在自定義工具箱中創建腳本工具
- 自定義帶有驗證功能的腳本工具行為

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geoprocessing_and_python/controlling-license-behavior.html

**text**　def isLicensed(self):
 """Allow the tool to run, only if the ArcGIS 3D Analyst extension
 is available."""
 try:
     if arcpy.CheckExtension("3D") != "Available":
         raise Exception
 except Exception:
     return False  # The tool cannot be run
 return True  # The tool can be run

**source_language**　zh-cn

## GVITM · 討論

**id**　9746ec6adcd4b65e65-2

**english**　GVITM

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 函數
- 波段索引

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/gvitm.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/gvitm.html

**text**　“綠色植被指數”(GVI) 方法設計源于 Landsat MSS 影像，并已針對 Landsat TM 影像進行了修改。 它也稱為 Landsat TM Tasseled Cap 綠色植被指數。 它可用于波段共享相同光譜特征的影像。
\(GVI=-0.2848 \space * \space Band1 \space - \space 0.2435 \space * \space Band2 \space - \space 0.5436 \space * \space Band3 \space + \space 0.7243 \space * \space Band4 \space + \space 0.084 \space * \space Band5 \space - \space 1.18 \space * \space Band7\)
有關其他多波段網格索引的信息，請參見 Band Arithmetic 網格函數。
網格對象所引用的網格資料集是臨時性的。 要將其設置為永久，可以調用網格對象的 save 方法。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/gvitm.html#discussion

## RemoveToolbox · 語法

**id**　97a39003aa7dee7da4-4

**english**　RemoveToolbox

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 工具和工具箱

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/removetoolbox.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/removetoolbox.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- toolbox
- The name of the toolbox, including either path or alias, to be removed from the current geoprocessing session. The name/path or alias should be placed in a double-quoted string. Server toolboxes can be removed using a semicolon delimiter. The name, including path, or alias, of the toolbox to be removed from the current geoprocessing session. Place the name/path, or alias, string inside double quotes. Server toolboxes can also be removed using a semicolon delimiter. Syntax for Internet ArcGIS Server** URL servername;{username};{password} Syntax for Local ArcGIS Server** machinename;servername. Syntax for Internet ArcGIS Server *URL;servername;{username};{password} * Syntax for Local ArcGIS Server machinename;servername
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/removetoolbox.html#syntax

## ListIndexes · 代碼示例

**id**　97e4037163236fa8a3-8

**english**　ListIndexes

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 列出資料

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/listindexes.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/listindexes.html

**text**　import arcpy

featureclass = "c:/data/roads.shp"

# Get list of indexes for roads.shp and print properties
indexes = arcpy.ListIndexes(featureclass)
for index in indexes:
    print("Name        : {0}".format(index.name))
    print("IsAscending : {0}".format(index.isAscending))
    print("IsUnique    : {0}".format(index.isUnique))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/listindexes.html#code-sample

## GenerateDirectionsFeatures · 匯總

**id**　98756cc7c54170846b-1

- 舊版本

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/generatedirectionsfeatures.html

**text**　在通過求解網絡分析圖層而生成的地圖及相應的文本方向上創建路徑線的圖徵類。 可使用網絡分析圖層的 StreetDirectionProperties 對象來修改輸出。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/network-analyst/generatedirectionsfeatures.html#summary

## GenerateDirectionsFeatures · 討論

**id**　98756cc7c54170846b-2

**english**　GenerateDirectionsFeatures

**chapter**　
- Python
- Network Analyst 模塊
- 舊版本
- 函數

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/generatedirectionsfeatures.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/generatedirectionsfeatures.html

**text**　多個 Network Analyst 求解程序將生成街道路徑作為輸出，并可有選擇地創建描述這些路徑的轉彎指示文本方向。 GenerateDirectionsFeatures 允許您生成這些文本方向，作為與地圖上含相應線圖徵的每一段文本方向相配對的圖徵類。
可使用網絡分析圖層上的 StreetDirectionProperties 設置來修改方向的輸出，例如語言、樣式和單位。
GenerateDirectionsFeatures 函數僅適用于路徑、車輛配送和最近設施點網絡分析圖層。 此外，分析圖層使用的網絡資料集必須配置為方向。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/network-analyst/generatedirectionsfeatures.html#discussion

## GenerateDirectionsFeatures · 代碼示例

**id**　98756cc7c54170846b-10

**english**　GenerateDirectionsFeatures

**chapter**　
- Python
- Network Analyst 模塊
- 舊版本
- 函數

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/generatedirectionsfeatures.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/generatedirectionsfeatures.html

**text**　#Get the route layer object from a layer named "Route" in the map
doc = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject('current')
map_obj = doc.listMaps()[0]
rt_layer = map_obj.listLayers('Route')[0]

#Generate directions features and save them to disk.
arcpy.na.GenerateDirectionsFeatures(rt_layer,
                                    'C:/Data/Directions.gdb/RouteDirections')

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/network-analyst/generatedirectionsfeatures.html#code-sample

## SearchNeighborhoodStandardCircular · 代碼示例

**id**　98904bf79353a0ce00-17

**english**　SearchNeighborhoodStandardCircular

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 地統計分析

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/searchneighborhoodstandardcircular-class-.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/searchneighborhoodstandardcircular-class-.html

**text**　# Name: EmpiricalBayesianKriging_Example_02.py
# Description: Bayesian kriging approach whereby many models created around the
#   semivariogram model estimated by the restricted maximum likelihood algorithm is used.
# Requirements: Geostatistical Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/gapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inPointFeatures = "ca_ozone_pts.shp"
zField = "ozone"
outLayer = "outEBK"
outRaster = "C:/gapyexamples/output/ebkout"
cellSize = 10000.0
transformation = "NONE"
maxLocalPoints = 50
overlapFactor = 0.5
numberSemivariograms = 100
# Set variables for search neighborhood
radius = 300000
angle = 0
maxNeighbors = 15
minNeighbors = 10
sectorType = "ONE_SECTOR"
searchNeighbourhood = arcpy.SearchNeighborhoodStandardCircular(radius,
                                                       angle, maxNeighbors,
                                                       minNeighbors, sectorType)
outputType = "PREDICTION"
quantileValue = ""
thresholdType = ""
probabilityThreshold = ""

# Run EmpiricalBayesianKriging
arcpy.ga.EmpiricalBayesianKriging(inPointFeatures, zField, outLayer, outRaster,
                                  cellSize, transformation, maxLocalPoints, overlapFactor, numberSemivariograms,
                                  searchNeighbourhood, outputType, quantileValue, thresholdType, probabilityThreshold)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/searchneighborhoodstandardcircular-class-.html#properties
